name: Gulf Coast Rental Co
description: Sarah and Tater Harris met on a summer's day in Lima, Peru, in 2010. Soon after, they embarked on a journey together to Tater's home state of Alabama, where they instantly fell in love with the charm of Orange Beach—its stunning coastline, incredible dining, and the vibrant, growing community. Five years later, they tied the knot and spent the next nine years traveling and living across the United States. Their adventures took them to places like the Caribbean, Chicago, Illinois, Ann Arbor, Michigan, Northern California, Lake Charles, Louisiana, Chattanooga, Tennessee, and Beaumont, Texas, among others. In 2016, after purchasing their first Polaris Slingshot, they discovered the thrill and freedom of open-air cruising. Wanting to share this exhilarating experience with others, they decided to start a rental business offering Polaris Slingshots, Jeep Wranglers, LSVs, and beach and baby equipment. With a commitment to providing an unforgettable experience, they set their prices to ensure both tourists and locals could enjoy a five-star adventure without breaking the bank. The idea for beach and baby equipment rental came later. While taking a vacation to Hawaii both Sarah and Tater noticed all of the rental companies that provided numerous items to the tourist that visited there. They realized that their own home town where they had kept their permanent address had no such place and was in need of a company that offered high quality beach and baby equipment at a great price. They also added overflow parking for all of the tourist and locals alike that are in need of additional parking in the season as well as the off season. Sarah, Tater and their son Jay are extremely thankful to be able to provide a five-star experience with high quality equipment for everyone to enjoy. They are looking forward to being here for our beloved tourist and amazing locals for years to come.

description: Sarah and Tater Harris met on a summer's day in Lima, Peru, in 2010. Soon after, they embarked on a journey together to Tater's home state of Alabama, where they instantly fell in love with the charm of Orange Beach—its stunning coastline, incredible dining, and the vibrant, growing community. Five years later, they tied the knot and spent the next nine years traveling and living across the United States. Their adventures took them to places like the Caribbean, Chicago, Illinois, Ann Arbor, Michigan, Northern California, Lake Charles, Louisiana, Chattanooga, Tennessee, and Beaumont, Texas, among others. In 2016, after purchasing their first Polaris Slingshot, they discovered the thrill and freedom of open-air cruising. Wanting to share this exhilarating experience with others, they decided to start a rental business offering Polaris Slingshots, Jeep Wranglers, LSVs, and beach and baby equipment. With a commitment to providing an unforgettable experience, they set their prices to ensure both tourists and locals could enjoy a five-star adventure without breaking the bank. The idea for beach and baby equipment rental came later. While taking a vacation to Hawaii both Sarah and Tater noticed all of the rental companies that provided numerous items to the tourist that visited there. They realized that their own home town where they had kept their permanent address had no such place and was in need of a company that offered high quality beach and baby equipment at a great price. They also added overflow parking for all of the tourist and locals alike that are in need of additional parking in the season as well as the off season. Sarah, Tater and their son Jay are extremely thankful to be able to provide a five-star experience with high quality equipment for everyone to enjoy. They are looking forward to being here for our beloved tourist and amazing locals for years to come.

description: Sarah and Tater Harris met on a summer's day in Lima, Peru, in 2010. Soon after, they embarked on a journey together to Tater's home state of Alabama, where they instantly fell in love with the charm of Orange Beach—its stunning coastline, incredible dining, and the vibrant, growing community. Five years later, they tied the knot and spent the next nine years traveling and living across the United States. Their adventures took them to places like the Caribbean, Chicago, Illinois, Ann Arbor, Michigan, Northern California, Lake Charles, Louisiana, Chattanooga, Tennessee, and Beaumont, Texas, among others. In 2016, after purchasing their first Polaris Slingshot, they discovered the thrill and freedom of open-air cruising. Wanting to share this exhilarating experience with others, they decided to start a rental business offering Polaris Slingshots, Jeep Wranglers, LSVs, and beach and baby equipment. With a commitment to providing an unforgettable experience, they set their prices to ensure both tourists and locals could enjoy a five-star adventure without breaking the bank. The idea for beach and baby equipment rental came later. While taking a vacation to Hawaii both Sarah and Tater noticed all of the rental companies that provided numerous items to the tourist that visited there. They realized that their own home town where they had kept their permanent address had no such place and was in need of a company that offered high quality beach and baby equipment at a great price. They also added overflow parking for all of the tourist and locals alike that are in need of additional parking in the season as well as the off season. Sarah, Tater and their son Jay are extremely thankful to be able to provide a five-star experience with high quality equipment for everyone to enjoy. They are looking forward to being here for our beloved tourist and amazing locals for years to come.

--- entity_offer [row 11 of 11] ---
offer_id: 13605
entity_slug: gulf-coast-rental-co
name: Gulf Coast Rental Co
description: Sarah and Tater Harris met on a summer's day in Lima, Peru, in 2010. Soon after, they embarked on a journey together to Tater's home state of Alabama, where they instantly fell in love with the charm of Orange Beach—its stunning coastline, incredible dining, and the vibrant, growing community. Five years later, they tied the knot and spent the next nine years traveling and living across the United States. Their adventures took them to places like the Caribbean, Chicago, Illinois, Ann Arbor, Michigan, Northern California, Lake Charles, Louisiana, Chattanooga, Tennessee, and Beaumont, Texas, among others. In 2016, after purchasing their first Polaris Slingshot, they discovered the thrill and freedom of open-air cruising. Wanting to share this exhilarating experience with others, they decided to start a rental business offering Polaris Slingshots, Jeep Wranglers, LSVs, and beach and baby equipment. With a commitment to providing an unforgettable experience, they set their prices to ensure both tourists and locals could enjoy a five-star adventure without breaking the bank. The idea for beach and baby equipment rental came later. While taking a vacation to Hawaii both Sarah and Tater noticed all of the rental companies that provided numerous items to the tourist that visited there. They realized that their own home town where they had kept their permanent address had no such place and was in need of a company that offered high quality beach and baby equipment at a great price. They also added overflow parking for all of the tourist and locals alike that are in need of additional parking in the season as well as the off season. Sarah, Tater and their son Jay are extremely thankful to be able to provide a five-star experience with high quality equipment for everyone to enjoy. They are looking forward to being here for our beloved tourist and amazing locals for years to come.

title: Gulf Coast Rental Co
category: rental
subtitle: Sarah and Tater Harris met on a summer's day in Lima, Peru, in 2010. Soon after, they embarked on a journey together to Tater's home state of Alabama, where they instantly fell in love with the charm of Orange Beach—its stunning coastline, incredible dining, and the vibrant, growing community. Five years later, they tied the knot and spent the next nine years traveling and living across the United States. Their adventures took them to places like the Caribbean, Chicago, Illinois, Ann Arbor, Michigan, Northern California, Lake Charles, Louisiana, Chattanooga, Tennessee, and Beaumont, Texas, among others. In 2016, after purchasing their first Polaris Slingshot, they discovered the thrill and freedom of open-air cruising. Wanting to share this exhilarating experience with others, they decided to start a rental business offering Polaris Slingshots, Jeep Wranglers, LSVs, and beach and baby equipment. With a commitment to providing an unforgettable experience, they set their prices to ensure both tourists and locals could enjoy a five-star adventure without breaking the bank. The idea for beach and baby equipment rental came later. While taking a vacation to Hawaii both Sarah and Tater noticed all of the rental companies that provided numerous items to the tourist that visited there. They realized that their own home town where they had kept their permanent address had no such place and was in need of a company that offered high quality beach and baby equipment at a great price. They also added overflow parking for all of the tourist and locals alike that are in need of additional parking in the season as well as the off season. Sarah, Tater and their son Jay are extremely thankful to be able to provide a five-star experience with high quality equipment for everyone to enjoy. They are looking forward to being here for our beloved tourist and amazing locals for years to come.
